The browser's localStorage stays as the local copy; the store wins when the page loads. function sldbAnnotationsConfig(config, search) {   const params = new URLSearchParams(search || '');   const server = (params.get('sldb') || (config && config.server) || '').replace(/\/+$/, '');   return { ...(config || {}), server, doc: params.get('annotations') || (config && config.doc) || '' }; }  function sldbAnnotationsStore(config, fetchImpl) {   let current = null;   const url = path => `${config.server}${path}`;    async function read() {     const response = await fetchImpl(url(`/document?id=${encodeURIComponent(config.doc)}`));     if (response.status === 404) {       current = null;       return [];     }     if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`sldb serve answered ${response.status} reading ${config.doc}`);     current = (await response.json()).payload;     return Array.isArray(current.annotations) ? current.annotations : [];   }    function change(items) {     if (!current) {       const payload = { title: config.title || config.doc, catalog: config.catalog || '', annotations: items };       return { id: config.doc, action: 'create', model: config.model, payload };     }     return { id: config.doc, action: 'update', model: config.model, payload: { ...current, annotations: items }, expected: current };   }    function post(items) {     const body = JSON.stringify({ changes: [change(items)] });     return fetchImpl(url('/save'), { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body });   }    function merge(stored, local) {     const seen = new Set();     return [...stored, ...local].filter(a => {       const key = JSON.stringify([a.key, a.comentario, a.ts]);       if (seen.has(key)) return false;       seen.add(key);       return true;     });   }    async function write(items) {     let response = await post(items);     if (response.status === 409) {       items = merge(await read(), items);       response = await post(items);     }     if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`sldb serve answered ${response.status} saving ${config.doc}: ${await response.text()}`);     await read();     return items;   }    return { read, write, merge }; }  function installSldbAnnotations(config, fetchImpl) {   if (!config.server || !config.doc || typeof annoWriteStore !== 'function') return null;   const store = sldbAnnotationsStore(config, fetchImpl);   const keepLocal = annoWriteStore;   let queue = Promise.resolve();   globalThis.annoWriteStore = function (items) {     keepLocal(items);     queue = queue       .then(() => store.write(items))       .then(saved => { if (saved !== items) { keepLocal(saved); annoRender(); } })       .catch(error => console.warn('[specir] annotations not saved in sldb:', error.message));     return queue;   };   globalThis.annoLoad = function () {     return store.read()       .then(items => { keepLocal(items); annoRender(); })       .catch(error => {         console.warn('[specir] annotations not read from sldb, using the local copy:', error.message);         annoCache = annoReadStore();         annoRender();       });   };   return store; }  installSldbAnnotations(sldbAnnotationsConfig(window.SPECIR_ANNOTATIONS, (globalThis.location || {}).search), globalThis.fetch && globalThis.fetch.bind(globalThis)); // </sldb-annotations> (async () => {   const steps = JSON.parse(process.argv[1]);
